# Draft: full main-to-dev integration with #7336 and #7469 for CI

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/7960) · state: open · merged: False

## Original PR description

## Purpose

Draft **for integration CI only; do not merge yet**. This carries the full pinned main branch into dev and provisionally includes #7336 and #7469. Both PRs are still open. The formal sync will be rebuilt/reconciled against their actual landed main commits after they merge.

## Frozen inputs

| Input | SHA |
| --- | --- |
| dev | b4407209223d290bbf7d6f2167d4e2ae51b7a58d |
| main | d5fbb65eceedbcab2cb7c425d37c10fcdbdc5bf6 |
| #7336: mHC PP/VPP | 1f2c81231cd2d8d306e58f267c72de3e053fa60d |
| #7469: packed DSv4 CP | 0f2ced4de25c6d46b927dc09a140b30b46bf74de |

This updates the previously inspected main-first candidate with 5 newer dev commits and 121 newer main commits, plus the two pending PR heads. It preserves full ancestry: the top-level commit has dev and a staged main-plus-PRs merge as its two parents. This is not a 100-commit slice.

## Resolution decisions

- **DSv4:** keep upstream static ModuleSpecs and wrapper-owned inverse RoPE. Add #7469's static contiguous packed THD/CP implementation (TP1; cuDNN packed backend). Dynamic CP, the legacy balanced indexer and compact MXFP8 indexer remain deferred; they are not silently re-enabled.
- **mHC / wide residual:** carry #7336's PP/VPP widths, viewless embedding-only output, empty-stage handling, deterministic GDN normalization and mixed-precision optimizer checkpoint fixes. Preserve dev's fixed-shape packed P2P and scheduled-layer boundaries. Main's wide-residual implementation remains separate from mHC.
- **MTP / CP:** adapt the main contiguous-roll contract to dev's grouped-field API, preserve per-field padding fills and differentiable HSM rolls, and keep explicit runtime process groups. Main's scoped TE CP binding replaces the old manual restore; legacy singleton metadata retains a compatibility path.
- **Muon / checkpointing:** retain dev's per-head QKV/MLA splitting, HDO resume-step fix and compact LayerWise path together with main's GTP/layer-sharded changes. QKV splitting happens after GTP reconstruction, not on a physical row shard.
- **MoE:** retain QB, DeepEP v2, NCCL-EP and dev metadata plumbing; reconcile main's hash-routing/replay and dense-route contracts. Padding exclusion belongs to the dispatcher; invalid dense routes retain zero weights.
- **Training / dependencies:** adopt main's authoritative profiling/logger/RL configuration, maintain dev builder extensions, and update TE/cuDNN-frontend/NVRx dependencies. The lockfile was regenerated, not manually merged.
- Preserve dev CODEOWNERS and existing dev golden values. Keep the incoming native tests and retain dev-specific PP tests separately, updating contracts that changed.

## Local validation

- `BASE_REF=dev CHECK_ONLY=true bash tools/autoformat.sh`: exit 0. Black, isort, Pylint and configured Ruff passed.
- The script's **non-gating mypy step did not pass**: it stopped parsing existing FSDP source. This is not being reported as a type-check pass.
- 1,050 changed Python files compiled; no duplicate function/class definitions remain in the inspected changeset.
- 15 targeted CPU checks passed for actual-source contiguous roll/mask/gradient behavior, DSv4 scope guards and mHC pipeline-width calculation.
- `git diff --check` passed.
- Dependency resolution completed with uv 0.7.2 inside `nvcr.io/nvidia/pytorch:26.08-py3`.

## Remaining qualification

No local CUDA/NCCL, TE/FLA/cuDNN/FlashMLA numerical, distributed model, or CUDA-graph qualification is claimed. The CPU checks use selected real function bodies and are not a full-package/GPU test run. In particular, mHC/PP/recompute, DSv4 packed CP/MTP, Muon+GTP and the new dependency combination still need exact-head CI.

Use the `Run tests` lightweight CI scope for this draft. This does not claim full functional/golden qualification. Further integration corrections may be needed before the formal sync.


## CI bring-up update

The first run at `2cbf9099e5` stopped at the separate golden-value schema gate; no GPU tests ran. All 79 offending files were existing dev goldens imported from main. The current draft applies the stated baseline-preservation rule to the complete candidate: all changed existing dev golden blobs are restored, while 36 new golden files remain and pass the **unchanged strict validator**. No values were relabelled as full precision and no validation was disabled.

The kernel-coverage gate also identified missing synchronization-era coverage. Added real bit-exact replay tests for DDP FP32 all-reduce/reduce-scatter, retained CP routing metadata, and DSA FP32-output projection dispatch/fallback, and registered them in the manifest. The coverage gate and the new tests' formatting/lint checks pass; native execution is pending CI.

Current test head: `f5217c1f10c5b3df021573cf47da3782e197bb6d`.
